Title: A Brief Account of the Life of Howell Harris, Esq; extracted from papers written by himself; to which is added a concise collection of his letters from the year 1738 to 1772

Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at |http: //books' removed (47 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Oxford University copy scanned by Google; Google's usage notice at the head of the OCR is removed; the long s is read as f throughout and the opening leaves carry scanner noise before the title page.
Content note: Harris's own account of his conversion in 1735, his first exhorting, the persecutions he met and the Trevecka family, extracted from his papers and printed at the Trevecka press, followed by a run of his letters with Whitefield and others from 1738 to 1772.
